    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/AGENTS.md",
      "sha256": "f4ca7baa33b857375464de26674c96f895a2966338cd0d4dd06585c71108bbf8",
      "bytes": 1734,
      "text": "# Working on this site\n\nRead `docs/story/handover.md` first, if it exists. Keep it current with the\nperson's words, decisions, implementation and check results. Keep all\nlasting context in this repository and commit it with the work.\n\nThis starter uses Next.js 16. Before using an unfamiliar API, read the\nrelevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` if provided by the installed\npackage, otherwise consult the official Next.js documentation for that\nversion. Do not assume older examples apply.\n\nInstall dependencies with `npm ci`, install Chromium with `npm run browsers`,\nthen run `npm test`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` and `npm run check`.\nThe last command builds and checks a production server. Open its screenshots\nbefore calling a visual change done. Read `README.md` for the content\ncontract and where the results are written.\n\nThe plain story is a test of the engine. Replace it from the person's\napproved storyboard and confirmed facts. Keep the text complete without\nthe scene, and test reduced motion, no WebGL and no JavaScript.\n\n<!-- BEGIN:nextjs-agent-rules -->\n\n# This is NOT the Next.js you know\n\nThis version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data. Read the relevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` (resolved from this file's directory; in monorepos the `next` package may not be visible from the repo root) before writing any code. Heed deprecation notices.\n\nThis block is written and re-added by `next dev` — verify at `node_modules/next/dist/server/lib/generate-agent-files.js`. Removing it from a diff only re-creates the uncommitted change; committing it with your work keeps the tree clean.\n\n<!-- END:nextjs-agent-rules -->\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/README.md",
      "sha256": "fb219637830adf6bec59c5d38c61d6ddd76c5bd3f997b420f91f2238d92e0b2f",
      "bytes": 8053,
      "text": "# Storyfolio engine\n\nA starting project for one scroll, one story and one family of 3D pieces.\nThe supplied page is a plain test story, not a person's portfolio.\n\nTaken from V3 at `163b8ceb302b7b4e5beeff8f1161f4cdc7663b15` and made\nindependent of that site's content, paper shapes and assets. The source\nproject is unchanged. Storyfolio's engine and generated SVGs are MIT licensed.\n\n## Start\n\nUse Node 22.18 or later with native TypeScript support. Scaffold this folder\nwith `node <installed-skill>/scripts/scaffold.mjs <site-folder>`. Existing\n`docs/story/` files are kept; any conflicting engine path aborts the copy.\nThen, in the new site folder:\n\n```bash\nnpm ci\nnpm run browsers\nnpm run dev\n```\n\nOpen the local address printed by Next.js. Append `?look=dark` to choose a\nlook and `?fps` to show the frame meter. The appearance switch preserves\nother query parameters, the fragment and the current scroll position.\nRead the frame meter on a real phone; software rendering in a headless\nbrowser does not measure phone performance.\n\nNever install dependencies inside an installed skill. The copied project\nbelongs to the person, has its own lockfile, and does not depend on the\nskill after scaffolding. System fonts make builds independent of remote\nfont services. Add licensed local files with `next/font/local` if needed;\nthe scene starts without waiting for them and redraws faces when fonts load.\n\n## From storyboard to content\n\n`lib/story/` contains pure, tested calculations. `components/story/` owns\nthe browser and scene. The person's story belongs in `content/`:\n\n| File | Storyboard input |\n|---|---|\n| `story.ts` | Chapter IDs, names, roles, beat IDs and screen heights; one fixed piece count |\n| `formations.ts` | A rest formation for every beat; optional `within` formation reached as its rest is scrolled |\n| `faces.ts` | Pictures or canvas drawing functions for a beat and piece |\n| `copy.ts` | Metadata, language, labels and complete HTML text |\n| `looks.ts` | Ground, text and surface colors, lights and matte/no floor |\n| `extras.ts` | Lazy imports of chapter-specific shapes, each in its own error boundary |\n| `links.ts` | Ordinary HTML links over a named piece at a named beat |\n| `checks.json` | Optional checks of capabilities this fixture exercises |\n\nUse `as const satisfies StoryDef` for the story and\n`satisfies Record<BeatId, BeatFormation>` for formations. `checkStory`\nalso rejects invalid IDs, heights, piece counts and face references at\nruntime. A formation contains one position, rotation and size per piece,\nplus framing. `layouts.ts` supplies stack, fan, ring, grid, row and seeded\nscatter; `measure` bounds their rotated faces. Depth, a custom shape's\nextent and a whole formation's turn can require extra framing room.\n\nThe HTML in `app/page.tsx` follows the same beat order using `Chapter` and\n`Beat`. A `Beat` can carry a static `fallback` image or diagram. Keep one\n`h1`, stable chapter heading IDs and every factual claim in HTML. The check\nscript verifies the rendered beat IDs against the content contract.\n\nThe bundle type is in `components/story/types.ts`. Import a bundle inside a\nclient component, as `content/experience.tsx` does, and pass server-rendered\nHTML as children. Bundles contain drawing functions and component loaders,\nso cannot be serialized across a server/client prop boundary.\n\nA custom `Piece` component receives its index, face material and look.\nBuild it at unit width/height centered at the origin. Its parent group gets\nthe formation's position, rotation and width/height scale each frame. Keep\nmaterials present from the first frame. Extras receive the shared frame,\nlook, reduced-motion flag and control store and mount only near their\nchapter. Their shapes are children of the formation group.\n\nUse `useExtraFrame` from `components/story/extra-frame.tsx` for an extra's\nframe callback. It receives the same state, delta and optional XR frame as\nReact Three Fiber's `useFrame`. A synchronous exception disables that\ncallback immediately and unmounts its owning extra, leaving the core scene,\nother extras and HTML running. Each extra also has a React boundary for\nrender failures and rejected lazy imports. For example:\n\n```tsx\nuseExtraFrame(() => {\n  if (line.current) line.current.visible = frameRef.current.from === 1;\n});\n```\n\n`content/extras/middle-line.tsx` uses this hook. A raw third-party `useFrame`\ncallback, timer, event handler or rejected asynchronous task does not pass\nthrough this guard. Handle failures at those boundaries explicitly;\nwrapping a component in a React boundary does not catch them.\n\nFor caption controls, use `useControl` or `useControls` from\n`components/story/controls.tsx` in a client component. Real controls remain\nHTML. The canvas takes no pointer events. Overlay links are hidden and\nremoved from keyboard navigation outside their beat; supply the same\ndestination in the text for fallback access.\n\nFor a sketch, add a client component importing its own bundle and HTML to\n`content/sketches/index.ts`. Its key becomes `/sketch/<id>`. The initial\nregistry is empty and unknown keys return 404.\n\n## Check\n\n```bash\nnpm test\nnpm run typecheck\nnpm run lint\nnpm run check\n```\n\n`check` builds, starts its own production server on a free local port,\nruns the browser matrix and stops only that process group. It writes a\nmanifest, logs, reports and screenshots under `checks/<timestamp>/` and\nreturns a failure exit code for failed checks. `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH`\nselects an optional shared browser cache. `PLAYWRIGHT_CORE` is an optional\noverride; normally the installed local package provides the browser tool.\n\nThe matrix checks both looks at wide, short and phone sizes, reduced\nmotion, no WebGL and no JavaScript. Optional plain-story probes exercise\nkeyboard access and failed chapter loading. Context loss checks the HTML\nfallback. Wide captures include the beginning, middle and end of each\nbeat's rest; reduced-motion captures also check that a stationary scene\nstays still. Open every screenshot for overlap, clipping, legibility and\nframing. Automatic passes do not replace visual review.\n\nFor a focused investigation use `npm run check -- --only=light-phone`.\n`--skip-build` checks a previously built app and is diagnostic only.\nNeither establishes a fresh full acceptance run. `scripts/shoot.mjs`\ndocuments individual screenshots, blocked requests, look switching,\noverflow and shader checks.\n\nNo JavaScript leaves the server-rendered text. No WebGL or a failed scene\nleaves ordinary text and any beat's static image. Reduced motion cuts\nbetween poses, including within-beat formations, without idle movement.\nThe scene pauses offscreen, caps sharpness on touch devices and steps down\nquality when measured frames stay slow.\n\n## Ada Mensah: current local spine\n\nThis project now contains the approved stop-4 spine: Scope, Transition and\nReview, in three appearances of the same six narrative rows. Start with\n`NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run dev`, then scroll Bench paper (default),\nInstrument paper (`?look=dark`) and Review sheet (`?look=review`). The other\nfour beats wait for the person's look choice. See `docs/story/handover.md`\nand `docs/story/review.md` for decisions and check evidence.\n\nThis fixture uses static HTML and supplied diagrams at widths up to 767px\nand under reduced motion. `content/checks.json` declares those checks. The\nsupplemental production check is `node scripts/check-spine.mjs`; it checks\nthat those modes fetch no scene chunk, plus deep links, assets and contact\nlinks. Run it after the fresh production build from `npm run check`.\n\nThe sandbox's empty `.git` placeholder is read-only. Local commits use the\nproject's `.storyfolio-git` directory instead:\n`git --git-dir=.storyfolio-git --work-tree=. log --oneline`.\n\nFor environments that limit the lifetime of one command, `npm run check --\n--jobs=3 --wait=1000` runs the same full production matrix in independent\nbrowser batches, preserving its reports and exit status. The default\nremains sequential. This does not measure device performance.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/app/globals.css",
      "sha256": "18b1febde7fdfb60ef381f72c5fa60dbd92e9e7bd30d447206528c3e2aeef9cb",
      "bytes": 5388,
      "text": "@import \"tailwindcss\";\n@import \"../components/story/story.css\";\n:root { font-family: system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", sans-serif; }\n* { box-sizing: border-box; }\nbody { margin: 0; }\na { color: inherit; }\nbutton, select { font: inherit; }\nhtml { scroll-behavior: auto; }\n.story { --font-story-display: system-ui, sans-serif; }\n.site-header { position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; z-index: 5; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 1.5rem; padding: 1.6rem 3.5vw; border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--story-ink), transparent 80%); pointer-events: auto; }\n.wordmark { font: 600 1rem ui-monospace, monospace; text-decoration: none; }\n.wordmark span { font-size: .7rem; font-weight: 400; }\n.header-contact { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 1.8rem; font-size: .75rem; }\n.header-contact .contact-links { margin: 0; gap: 1.8rem; font-size: .75rem; }\n.contact-links { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 1.8rem; margin-top: 1.4rem; font-size: .9rem; }\n.contact-links a { text-decoration: none; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--story-accent); padding-block: .5rem; }\n.file-type { font: .6rem ui-monospace, monospace; color: var(--story-faint); margin-left: .4rem; }\n.spine-nav { position: absolute; top: 6.5rem; left: 3.5vw; z-index: 5; display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 1.2rem; font: .7rem ui-monospace, monospace; pointer-events: auto; }\n.spine-nav a { text-decoration: none; }\n.spine-nav span { color: var(--story-faint); }\n.story-bar { top: 8.5rem; height: calc(100lvh - 8.5rem); margin-bottom: calc(-100lvh + 8.5rem); padding-inline: 3.5vw; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-bar { top: 8.5rem; }\n.story-caption { padding: 3rem 5vw; }\n.story-caption-text { max-width: min(38vw, 34rem); }\n.story-title { font-size: clamp(2.2rem, 4.8vw, 5rem); font-weight: 550; letter-spacing: -.055em; line-height: 1.05; }\n.opening-question { font-size: clamp(1.2rem, 1.8vw, 1.65rem); line-height: 1.3; max-width: 26rem; margin: 1.2rem 0; letter-spacing: -.025em; }\n.story-body { font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.6; }\n.story-note { font-size: .85rem; line-height: 1.55; }\n.project-label { font: .7rem ui-monospace, monospace; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .04em; color: var(--story-accent); margin-top: 1.3rem; }\n.diagram-preview { margin: 1.5rem 0 0; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 6.5rem 1fr; column-gap: 1rem; align-items: center; padding-top: 1rem; border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--story-faint), transparent 55%); }\n.diagram-preview img { grid-row: 1 / 3; width: 100%; height: auto; border-radius: 2px; }\n.diagram-preview figcaption { font-size: .7rem; line-height: 1.4; color: var(--story-faint); }\n.diagram-preview a { font-size: .7rem; padding-block: .6rem; text-underline-offset: .25rem; }\n.story-rail { right: 2vw; }\n.story-rail a { font-size: .65rem; }\n.back-link { display: inline-block; font-size: .75rem; margin-top: 1.5rem; text-underline-offset: .3rem; }\n.site-footer { position: relative; padding: 1.5rem 3.5vw; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 1rem; border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--story-faint), transparent 60%); font: .7rem ui-monospace, monospace; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] { padding-top: 14rem; padding-bottom: 0; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-caption-text { max-width: 45rem; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-caption { padding: 3rem max(5vw, calc((100vw - 55rem)/2)); }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-beat { border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--story-faint), transparent 70%); }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .diagram-preview { display: block; max-width: 32rem; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .diagram-preview img { width: 100%; margin-bottom: 1rem; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .diagram-preview figcaption { font-size: .85rem; }\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .diagram-preview a { display: inline-block; font-size: .85rem; }\n.missing-page { min-height: 100vh; padding: 15vh 10vw; background: #f2efe6; color: #282b29; }\n@media (max-width: 767px) {\n  .site-header { padding: 1.2rem; align-items: flex-start; }\n  .wordmark span { display: block; margin-top: .4rem; font-size: .6rem; }\n  .header-contact { display: block; }\n  .header-contact .contact-links { display: block; }\n  .header-contact .contact-links a { display: block; padding: .4rem 0; }\n  .header-contact > a { display: inline-block; margin-top: .6rem; }\n  .spine-nav { top: 9rem; left: 1.2rem; right: 1.2rem; gap: 1rem; }\n  .spine-nav span { width: 100%; }\n  .story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-bar { top: 13.5rem; padding: 0 1.2rem; justify-content: flex-start; }\n  .story-look-switch { flex-wrap: wrap; border-radius: .3rem; }\n  .story-look-switch button { padding: .5rem .6rem; font-size: .7rem; border-radius: .2rem; }\n  .story[data-scene=\"off\"] { padding-top: 18rem; }\n  .story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-caption { padding: 2.5rem 1.2rem; }\n  .story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-caption-text { max-width: 100%; }\n  .story-title { font-size: 2.8rem; }\n  .site-footer { padding: 1.5rem 1.2rem; flex-direction: column; }\n}\n/* Static content starts below the header; absolute navigation belongs to the story root. */\n.story[data-scene=\"off\"] .story-content { position: static; }\n/* Keep the appearance control above the rows on shorter desktop screens. */\n.story[data-scene=\"on\"] .story-bar { top: 5rem; height: calc(100lvh - 5rem); margin-bottom: calc(-100lvh + 5rem); }\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/app/not-found.tsx",
      "sha256": "744440f19bb101c269d33dd37facbdfb6e35c5516a4099550ccdec6976081af9",
      "bytes": 251,
      "text": "import Link from \"next/link\";\nexport default function NotFound() { return <main className=\"missing-page\"><p>404 / Missing address</p><h1>This page is not here.</h1><Link href=\"/\">Home</Link> · <a href=\"mailto:ada@example.com\">Email Ada</a></main>; }\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/app/page.tsx",
      "sha256": "20a6b99fda69bea1fa89fa81a207a6f95ca7bd5cff9f6822d2aeb4a8cb4d3c5e",
      "bytes": 3188,
      "text": "import { Chapter, Beat, Kicker, Title, Body, Note } from \"@/components/story/chapter\";\nimport { Experience } from \"@/content/experience\";\nimport { story } from \"@/content/story\";\nimport { copy } from \"@/content/copy\";\nconst contact = <div className=\"contact-links\"><a href=\"mailto:ada@example.com\">Email Ada <span aria-hidden=\"true\">↗</span></a><a href=\"/ada-mensah-cv.html\" download>Download CV <span className=\"file-type\">HTML ↓</span></a></div>;\nfunction Diagram({ fixture = false }: { fixture?: boolean }) {\n  return <figure className=\"diagram-preview\">\n    {/* Supplied local drawings have intrinsic dimensions and need no optimizer. */}\n    {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}\n    <img src={fixture ? \"/assets/bring-up-fixture.svg\" : \"/assets/power-states.svg\"} width=\"720\" height=\"620\" alt={fixture ? \"Explanatory bring-up diagram linking firmware command, a generic board, the fixture and a bench observation. It contains no circuit detail.\" : \"Explanatory diagram: Sleep leads to Measure, then Transmit, then back to Sleep. No durations or measured current values are shown.\"} />\n    <figcaption>{fixture ? \"Explanatory schematic, not a photograph or a circuit diagram.\" : \"Explanatory schematic, not a measured trace or a firmware specification.\"}</figcaption>\n    <a href={fixture ? \"/assets/bring-up-fixture.svg\" : \"/assets/power-states.svg\"}>{fixture ? \"View fixture schematic\" : \"View power-state schematic\"} <span aria-hidden=\"true\">↗</span></a>\n  </figure>;\n}\nexport default function Home() {\n  return <main id=\"content\"><a className=\"story-skip\" href=\"#scope\">Skip to content</a><Experience>\n    <header className=\"site-header\"><a className=\"wordmark\" href=\"#scope\">AM<span> / STATE BY STATE</span></a><div className=\"header-contact\">{contact}<a href=\"#review\">Skip to contact</a></div></header>\n    <nav className=\"spine-nav\" aria-label=\"Spine chapters\"><a href=\"#scope\">01 Scope</a><a href=\"#transition\">04 Transition</a><a href=\"#review\">06 Review</a><span>Spine study · 3 of 7 beats</span></nav>\n    {story.chapters.map((chapter, index) => <Chapter key={chapter.id} id={chapter.id}>\n      {chapter.beats.map(beat => { const words = copy.beats[beat.id]; return <Beat key={beat.id} story={story} id={beat.id}>\n        <Kicker number={chapter.number}>{chapter.name}</Kicker>\n        <Title id={`${chapter.id}-title`} as={index === 0 ? \"h1\" : \"h2\"}>{words.title}</Title>\n        {index === 0 && <p className=\"opening-question\">How does a firmware decision become a measurable signal?</p>}\n        <Body>{words.body}</Body><Note>{words.note}</Note>\n        {index === 0 && <><p className=\"project-label\">Mosslink / Environmental sensor</p>{contact}<Diagram fixture /></>}\n        {index === 1 && <Diagram />}\n        {index === 2 && <><Note>The longest run was 72 hours.</Note><Note>One preliminary emissions check passed. Full qualification is unfinished.</Note>{contact}<a className=\"back-link\" href=\"#scope\">Back to Scope ↑</a></>}\n      </Beat>; })}\n    </Chapter>)}\n    <footer className=\"site-footer\">Ada Mensah · Firmware and the bench <span>Local spine review / Choose an appearance above</span></footer>\n  </Experience></main>;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/components/story/faces.ts",
      "sha256": "10eced0f0e306a0d9c812348ace89f42b4c1c253e498407471dffe7eb9c232da",
      "bytes": 3066,
      "text": "import * as THREE from \"three\";\nimport type { Look } from \"@/lib/story/looks\";\nimport type { FaceDef } from \"./types\";\n\n/** Drawing is queued; at most two faces are painted by one animation frame. */\nexport function createFaces(faces: readonly FaceDef[], look: Look, coarse: boolean) {\n  let disposed = false;\n  const value = look.surface.glows ? 0 : 255;\n  const blank = new THREE.DataTexture(new Uint8Array([value, value, value, 255]), 1, 1);\n  blank.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;\n  blank.needsUpdate = true;\n  const made = new Map<string, { canvas: HTMLCanvasElement; texture: THREE.CanvasTexture }>();\n  const images = new Map<string, HTMLImageElement>();\n  const loading: HTMLImageElement[] = [];\n  const waiting = [...faces];\n  const queued = new Set(faces.map(face => face.id));\n  const queue = (face: FaceDef) => { if (!disposed && !queued.has(face.id)) { waiting.push(face); queued.add(face.id); } };\n  for (const face of faces) if (face.src) {\n    const picture = new Image();\n    loading.push(picture);\n    picture.crossOrigin = \"anonymous\";\n    picture.onload = () => { if (!disposed) { images.set(face.id, picture); queue(face); } };\n    picture.onerror = () => { /* The background and any canvas drawing remain readable. */ };\n    picture.src = face.src;\n  }\n  void document.fonts.ready.then(() => { if (!disposed) faces.forEach(queue); });\n  return {\n    blank,\n    get(beat: string, piece: number) {\n      const face = faces.find(item => item.beat === beat && item.piece === piece);\n      return face ? made.get(face.id)?.texture ?? blank : blank;\n    },\n    draw() {\n      for (let n = 0; n < 2 && waiting.length && !disposed; n++) {\n        const face = waiting.shift()!;\n        queued.delete(face.id);\n        let record = made.get(face.id);\n        if (!record) {\n          const canvas = document.createElement(\"canvas\");\n          canvas.width = coarse ? 384 : 768; canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(canvas.width / (face.aspect ?? .75)));\n          const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);\n          texture.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;\n          record = { canvas, texture }; made.set(face.id, record);\n        }\n        const { canvas, texture } = record;\n        const context = canvas.getContext(\"2d\");\n        if (!context) continue;\n        context.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n        context.fillStyle = look.surface.glows ? \"#000000\" : look.surface.color;\n        context.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n        const picture = images.get(face.id);\n        if (picture) context.drawImage(picture, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);\n        context.save();\n        face.draw?.(context, canvas.width, canvas.height, look);\n        context.restore();\n        texture.needsUpdate = true;\n      }\n    },\n    dispose() {\n      disposed = true;\n      for (const image of loading) { image.onload = null; image.onerror = null; }\n      images.clear();\n      for (const { texture } of made.values()) texture.dispose();\n      made.clear(); waiting.length = 0; blank.dispose();\n    },\n  };\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/components/story/story.tsx",
      "sha256": "de8782eb09fa55083ba63e199ce217dafda6790e2c740e8a0d809fe137f1c442",
      "bytes": 6167,
      "text": "\"use client\";\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from \"react\";\nimport dynamic from \"next/dynamic\";\nimport type { Stage } from \"@/lib/story/camera\";\nimport { createControls } from \"@/lib/story/controls\";\nimport { playhead, type BeatBox, type Playhead } from \"@/lib/story/timeline\";\nimport { useReducedMotion } from \"./use-reduced-motion\";\nimport { useWebGLSupported } from \"./use-webgl-supported\";\nimport { useLook } from \"./use-look\";\nimport { WebGLBoundary } from \"./webgl-boundary\";\nimport { LookSwitch } from \"./look-switch\";\nimport { ChapterRail } from \"./rail\";\nimport { Overlay } from \"./overlay\";\nimport { Meter } from \"./meter\";\nimport { ControlsProvider } from \"./controls\";\nimport { checkBundle } from \"./validate\";\nimport type { StoryBundle } from \"./types\";\nconst Scene = dynamic(() => import(\"./scene\"), { ssr: false });\n\nconst staticQuery = \"(max-width: 767px), (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)\";\nfunction subscribeStatic(notify: () => void) {\n  const media = window.matchMedia(staticQuery);\n  media.addEventListener(\"change\", notify);\n  return () => media.removeEventListener(\"change\", notify);\n}\nexport function Story({ bundle, children }: { bundle: StoryBundle; children: ReactNode }) {\n  const issues = useMemo(() => checkBundle(bundle), [bundle]);\n  if (issues.length) throw new Error(`Invalid story: ${issues.join(\"; \")}`);\n  const rootRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const headRef = useRef<Playhead>({ beat: 0, dwell: 0 });\n  const stageRef = useRef<Stage>({ viewport: 0, header: 0, captions: [] });\n  const pointerRef = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });\n  const fpsRef = useRef(0);\n  const [controls] = useState(createControls);\n  const staticMode = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeStatic, () => window.matchMedia(staticQuery).matches, () => true);\n  const webgl = useWebGLSupported();\n  const still = useReducedMotion();\n  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);\n  const [running, setRunning] = useState(true);\n  const [activeChapter, setActiveChapter] = useState<string | undefined>(bundle.story.chapters[0]?.id);\n  const onError = useCallback(() => setFailed(true), []);\n  const id = useLook(bundle.looks, bundle.defaultLook);\n  const look = bundle.looks.find(look => look.id === id) ?? bundle.looks[0];\n  const on = !staticMode && webgl === true && !failed;\n  const items = useMemo(() => bundle.story.chapters.map(chapter => ({ id: chapter.id, number: chapter.number, label: chapter.name })), [bundle.story]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const root = rootRef.current;\n    if (!root) return;\n    let boxes: BeatBox[] = [];\n    const update = () => { headRef.current = playhead(-root.getBoundingClientRect().top, window.innerHeight, boxes); };\n    const measure = () => {\n      const origin = root.getBoundingClientRect().top;\n      const beats = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\"[data-beat]\"));\n      boxes = beats.map(beat => { const box = beat.getBoundingClientRect(); return { top: box.top - origin, height: box.height }; });\n      const top = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>(\"[data-story-top]\");\n      stageRef.current = { viewport: window.innerHeight, header: top ? top.offsetTop + top.offsetHeight : 0, captions: beats.map(beat => {\n        const caption = beat.querySelector<HTMLElement>(\".story-caption\");\n        const text = beat.querySelector<HTMLElement>(\".story-caption-text\");\n        return caption && text ? text.getBoundingClientRect().height + parseFloat(getComputedStyle(caption).paddingBottom) : 0;\n      }) };\n      update();\n    };\n    measure();\n    const resize = new ResizeObserver(measure);\n    resize.observe(root);\n    root.querySelectorAll(\".story-caption-text\").forEach(node => resize.observe(node));\n    let visible = true;\n    const visibility = () => setRunning(visible && !document.hidden);\n    const seen = new IntersectionObserver(([entry]) => { visible = entry.isIntersecting; visibility(); });\n    seen.observe(root);\n    document.addEventListener(\"visibilitychange\", visibility);\n    window.addEventListener(\"scroll\", update, { passive: true });\n    window.addEventListener(\"resize\", measure);\n    return () => { resize.disconnect(); seen.disconnect(); window.removeEventListener(\"scroll\", update); window.removeEventListener(\"resize\", measure); document.removeEventListener(\"visibilitychange\", visibility); };\n  }, [bundle.story, on]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (still) { pointerRef.current = { x: 0, y: 0 }; return; }\n    const move = (event: PointerEvent) => { if (event.pointerType === \"mouse\") pointerRef.current = { x: event.clientX / window.innerWidth * 2 - 1, y: 1 - event.clientY / window.innerHeight * 2 }; };\n    window.addEventListener(\"pointermove\", move, { passive: true });\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"pointermove\", move);\n  }, [still]);\n  const style = { \"--story-ground\": look.ground, \"--story-ink\": look.ink, \"--story-faint\": look.faint, \"--story-accent\": look.accent } as CSSProperties;\n  return <div ref={rootRef} className=\"story\" data-scene={on ? \"on\" : \"off\"} data-look={look.id} style={style}>\n    <noscript><style>{\".story-look-switch, .story-control { display: none !important; }\"}</style></noscript>\n    {on ? <><div className=\"story-scene\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><WebGLBoundary onError={onError}><Scene bundle={bundle} headRef={headRef} stageRef={stageRef} pointerRef={pointerRef} controls={controls} look={look} still={still} running={running} rootRef={rootRef} fpsRef={fpsRef} onError={onError} onChapter={setActiveChapter} /></WebGLBoundary></div><div className=\"story-rail-holder\"><ChapterRail label={bundle.copy.railLabel} items={items} active={activeChapter} /></div><Overlay links={bundle.links ?? []} controls={controls} /></> : null}\n    {bundle.looks.length > 1 ? <div className=\"story-bar\"><div data-story-top><LookSwitch looks={bundle.looks} active={look.id} defaultLook={bundle.defaultLook ?? bundle.looks[0].id} label={bundle.copy.lookLabel} /></div></div> : null}\n    <div className=\"story-content\"><ControlsProvider controls={controls} sceneOff={!on}>{children}</ControlsProvider></div><Meter value={fpsRef} label={bundle.copy.fpsLabel} />\n  </div>;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/components/story/types.ts",
      "sha256": "29e064282d8669ff55863b5ddc20fdc477877fb03d9e8b30db8d926883a0c9b9",
      "bytes": 1421,
      "text": "import type { ComponentType, RefObject } from \"react\";\nimport type { MeshStandardMaterial } from \"three\";\nimport type { BeatFormation } from \"@/lib/story/formation\";\nimport type { StoryDef } from \"@/lib/story/story\";\nimport type { Look } from \"@/lib/story/looks\";\nimport type { Controls } from \"@/lib/story/controls\";\nimport type { Frame } from \"./conductor\";\n\nexport type FaceDef = {\n  id: string;\n  beat: string;\n  piece: number;\n  src?: string;\n  /** Printed width / height; defaults to the starter portrait card ratio. */\n  aspect?: number;\n  draw?: (context: CanvasRenderingContext2D, width: number, height: number, look: Look) => void;\n};\nexport type StoryLink = { beat: string; piece: number; href: string; label: string };\n/** Frame callbacks in optional extras use useExtraFrame to contain failures. */\nexport type ExtraProps = { frameRef: RefObject<Frame>; look: Look; still: boolean; controls: Controls };\nexport type PieceProps = { index: number; material: MeshStandardMaterial; look: Look };\nexport type StoryBundle = {\n  story: StoryDef;\n  formations: Record<string, BeatFormation>;\n  faces: readonly FaceDef[];\n  looks: readonly Look[];\n  defaultLook?: string;\n  copy: { railLabel: string; lookLabel: string; fpsLabel?: string };\n  extras?: readonly { chapter: string; load: () => Promise<{ default: ComponentType<ExtraProps> }> }[];\n  links?: readonly StoryLink[];\n  Piece?: ComponentType<PieceProps>;\n};\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/components/story/validate.test.ts",
      "sha256": "6ee648de16b8f38ebf1f3701b2658e92b0e60a76137b6db1ca54bf601038eb19",
      "bytes": 2610,
      "text": "import test from \"node:test\";\nimport assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport { blankFormation } from \"../../lib/story/formation.ts\";\nimport type { StoryBundle } from \"./types.ts\";\nimport { checkBundle } from \"./validate.ts\";\n\nfunction bundle(): StoryBundle {\n  return {\n    story: { pieces: 1, chapters: [{ id: \"opening\", number: 0, name: \"Opening\", role: \"opening\", beats: [{ id: \"opening.one\", height: 1 }] }] },\n    formations: { \"opening.one\": { rest: blankFormation(1) } },\n    faces: [{ id: \"first\", beat: \"opening.one\", piece: 0, src: \"/face.svg\" }],\n    looks: [{ id: \"light\", ground: \"#fff\", ink: \"#111\", faint: \"#555\", accent: \"#a00\", surface: { color: \"#fff\", roughness: 1, glows: false }, lights: { ambient: 1, key: 1, fill: 0, rim: 0 }, floor: \"none\", shadows: false }],\n    copy: { railLabel: \"Chapters\", lookLabel: \"Look\" },\n  };\n}\ntest(\"accepts a complete bundle without optional extras or links\", () => {\n  assert.deepEqual(checkBundle(bundle()), []);\n});\ntest(\"rejects face identities that would share or overwrite the wrong texture\", () => {\n  const value = bundle();\n  value.faces = [...value.faces, { ...value.faces[0] }];\n  const problems = checkBundle(value);\n  assert.ok(problems.includes(\"face id used twice: first\"));\n  assert.ok(problems.includes(\"two faces assigned to opening.one:0\"));\n});\ntest(\"rejects an absent look before hydration tries to read it\", () => {\n  const value = bundle();\n  value.looks = [];\n  value.defaultLook = \"missing\";\n  assert.ok(checkBundle(value).includes(\"bundle must have a look\"));\n  assert.ok(checkBundle(value).includes(\"default look is missing: missing\"));\n});\ntest(\"rejects links and extras that could never appear\", () => {\n  const value = bundle();\n  value.links = [{ beat: \"missing\", piece: 2, href: \"\", label: \"\" }];\n  value.extras = [{ chapter: \"missing\", load: async () => ({ default: () => null }) }];\n  const problems = checkBundle(value);\n  assert.ok(problems.includes(\"link names missing beat: missing\"));\n  assert.ok(problems.includes(\"link names missing piece: 2\"));\n  assert.ok(problems.includes(\"link must have a label and destination\"));\n  assert.ok(problems.includes(\"extra names missing chapter: missing\"));\n});\n\ntest(\"bounds custom face proportions before allocating a texture\", () => {\n  for (const aspect of [0, NaN, Infinity, .01, 100]) {\n    const value = bundle(); value.faces = [{ ...value.faces[0], aspect }];\n    assert.ok(checkBundle(value).some(problem => problem.includes(\"face aspect\")));\n  }\n  const value = bundle(); value.faces = [{ ...value.faces[0], aspect: 8 }];\n  assert.deepEqual(checkBundle(value), []);\n});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/components/story/validate.ts",
      "sha256": "8b5754eed78ef77be47067649d9fdc2e34bdff48eda5e785129e2db42d4b1915",
      "bytes": 2154,
      "text": "import { beatsOf, checkStory } from \"../../lib/story/story.ts\";\nimport type { StoryBundle } from \"./types.ts\";\n\n/** Validate the parts of a bundle that are not part of the pure story data. */\nexport function checkBundle(bundle: StoryBundle): string[] {\n  const problems = checkStory(bundle.story, bundle.formations, bundle.faces);\n  const beats = new Set(beatsOf(bundle.story).map(beat => beat.id));\n  const chapters = new Set(bundle.story.chapters.map(chapter => chapter.id));\n  const unique = (ids: readonly string[], kind: string) => {\n    const seen = new Set<string>();\n    for (const id of ids) {\n      if (!id.trim()) problems.push(`${kind} id must not be empty`);\n      if (seen.has(id)) problems.push(`${kind} id used twice: ${id}`);\n      seen.add(id);\n    }\n  };\n  unique(bundle.faces.map(face => face.id), \"face\");\n  unique(bundle.looks.map(look => look.id), \"look\");\n  if (!bundle.looks.length) problems.push(\"bundle must have a look\");\n  if (bundle.defaultLook && !bundle.looks.some(look => look.id === bundle.defaultLook)) problems.push(`default look is missing: ${bundle.defaultLook}`);\n  const assigned = new Set<string>();\n  for (const face of bundle.faces) {\n    if (face.aspect !== undefined && (!Number.isFinite(face.aspect) || face.aspect < 1/16 || face.aspect > 16)) problems.push(`face aspect must be between 1/16 and 16: ${face.id}`);\n    const key = `${face.beat}:${face.piece}`;\n    if (assigned.has(key)) problems.push(`two faces assigned to ${key}`);\n    assigned.add(key);\n    if (!face.src && !face.draw) problems.push(`face has no source or drawing: ${face.id}`);\n  }\n  for (const link of bundle.links ?? []) {\n    if (!beats.has(link.beat)) problems.push(`link names missing beat: ${link.beat}`);\n    if (!Number.isInteger(link.piece) || link.piece < 0 || link.piece >= bundle.story.pieces) problems.push(`link names missing piece: ${link.piece}`);\n    if (!link.label.trim() || !link.href.trim()) problems.push(\"link must have a label and destination\");\n  }\n  for (const extra of bundle.extras ?? []) if (!chapters.has(extra.chapter)) problems.push(`extra names missing chapter: ${extra.chapter}`);\n  return problems;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/checks.json",
      "sha256": "949837d672145b219e4053001438a447d991c64d432f9385abe2003892693d90",
      "bytes": 47,
      "text": "{ \"staticPhone\": true, \"staticReduced\": true }\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/copy.ts",
      "sha256": "87cfb969d607190b00b3a2d9bc76b26e1784ef885a5ca16770cb5d7fc214856d",
      "bytes": 1290,
      "text": "export const copy = {\n  lang: \"en\",\n  metadata: {\n    title: \"Ada Mensah — Firmware and the bench\",\n    description: \"Firmware, power-state scheduling and the bring-up fixture: Ada Mensah's contribution to Mosslink, with the evidence limits visible.\",\n    openGraph: { title: \"Ada Mensah — Firmware and the bench\", description: \"How does a firmware decision become a measurable signal?\", images: [\"/assets/share-card.svg\"] },\n  },\n  railLabel: \"Chapters\", lookLabel: \"Appearance\", fpsLabel: \"Frames per second\",\n  beats: {\n    \"scope.opening\": { title: \"Ada Mensah\", body: \"Firmware is code that controls a physical device.\", note: \"I wrote the firmware, scheduled the power states and built the bring-up fixture.\" },\n    \"transition.diagram\": { title: \"Reading transitions\", body: \"Sleep → Measure → Transmit → Sleep. This teaching loop explains a transition between states.\", note: \"Explanatory schematic, not a measured trace or a firmware specification.\" },\n    \"review.close\": { title: \"Code beside evidence\", body: \"I connect firmware decisions to bench observations through power-state scheduling and the bring-up fixture, while keeping the evidence limits visible.\", note: \"Two years is a projection from bench current readings and an assumed reporting cycle.\" },\n  },\n};\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/extras.ts",
      "sha256": "6c738050d7a8fc2e6bb214a401994e6a69ade3c052a263f45749699ffc5fc454",
      "bytes": 26,
      "text": "export const extras = [];\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/faces.ts",
      "sha256": "28249baf7550bb8cfdfe4a8668806ccda6a94fdeb811a3d3c9be58cf91598811",
      "bytes": 1426,
      "text": "import type { FaceDef } from \"@/components/story/types\";\nimport { story } from \"./story\";\nconst table = [\"Scope / Firmware\", \"Responsibilities / Ada + colleague\", \"State / Power scheduling\", \"Transition / Teaching diagram\", \"Observation / Bench evidence\", \"Review / Evidence limits\"];\nconst transition = [\"Sleep\", \"→ Measure\", \"Measure\", \"→ Transmit\", \"Transmit\", \"→ Sleep\"];\nexport const faces: FaceDef[] = story.chapters.flatMap(chapter => chapter.beats.flatMap(beat => Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, piece) => ({\n  id: `${beat.id}-${piece}`, beat: beat.id, piece, aspect: beat.id === \"transition.diagram\" ? 2/.42 : 3.65/.46,\n  draw(ctx, width, height, look) {\n    ctx.scale(width / 1000, height / (beat.id === \"transition.diagram\" ? 210 : 126));\n    width = 1000; height = beat.id === \"transition.diagram\" ? 210 : 126;\n    ctx.fillStyle = look.surface.color; ctx.fillRect(0,0,width,height);\n    ctx.strokeStyle = look.faint; ctx.lineWidth = 1;\n    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(width*.13,0); ctx.lineTo(width*.13,height); ctx.moveTo(0,height*.93); ctx.lineTo(width,height*.93); ctx.stroke();\n    ctx.fillStyle = look.accent; ctx.font = `${height*.24}px monospace`; ctx.fillText(String(piece+1).padStart(2,\"0\"),width*.035,height*.58);\n    ctx.fillStyle = look.ink; ctx.font = `${height*.27}px system-ui`; ctx.fillText(beat.id === \"transition.diagram\" ? transition[piece] : table[piece],width*.17,height*.58);\n  },\n}))));\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/formations.ts",
      "sha256": "8ea3a7491fe905c99ba22d4cba78cd81e57a2cbea630a2cbd9b56651f5842ba4",
      "bytes": 1156,
      "text": "import { blankFormation, type BeatFormation, type Formation, type Pose } from \"../lib/story/formation.ts\";\nimport { measure } from \"../lib/story/layouts.ts\";\nimport { story, type BeatId } from \"./story.ts\";\nfunction formation(pieces: Pose[], turn: [number, number] = [-.08, -.16]): Formation {\n  const bounds = measure(pieces);\n  return { ...blankFormation(story.pieces), pieces, span: bounds.span * 1.2, rise: bounds.rise * 1.05, anchor: [.45, .02], turn, near: 5, floor: -4 };\n}\nfunction table(depth: number): Pose[] {\n  return Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => ({ position: [0, (2.5-i)*.53, i*depth], rotation: [0,0,0], size: [3.65,.46] }));\n}\nconst loop: Pose[] = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => {\n  const p = [[-1.15,1.15],[1.15,1.15],[0,-1.05]][Math.floor(i/2)];\n  return { position: [p[0],p[1]-(i%2)*.48,0], rotation: [0,0,0], size: [2,.42] };\n});\nexport const formations = {\n  \"scope.opening\": { rest: formation(table(0)), within: formation(table(.045)) },\n  \"transition.diagram\": { rest: formation(loop, [0,-.08]) },\n  \"review.close\": { rest: formation(table(.045)), within: formation(table(0)) },\n} satisfies Record<BeatId, BeatFormation>;\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/links.ts",
      "sha256": "3f3dc7e34bfd5bd30d3b5d3e5105f038eff3ea5f804f9bb9bcc0a547b80b96cb",
      "bytes": 25,
      "text": "export const links = [];\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/looks.ts",
      "sha256": "2999cc2ade2a5f7905d645d890d58e22fb435128ac40a27705e5d94fae50834c",
      "bytes": 880,
      "text": "import type { Look } from \"../lib/story/looks.ts\";\nexport const looks = [\n  { id: \"light\", name: \"Bench paper\", ground: \"#f2efe6\", ink: \"#282b29\", faint: \"#62635c\", accent: \"#87551c\", surface: { color: \"#fffaf0\", roughness: 1, glows: false }, lights: { ambient: 1.5, key: 2, fill: .5, rim: .6 }, floor: \"none\", shadows: true },\n  { id: \"dark\", name: \"Instrument paper\", ground: \"#202523\", ink: \"#f4f0e5\", faint: \"#bbbeb4\", accent: \"#e5b875\", surface: { color: \"#353c38\", roughness: 1, glows: false }, lights: { ambient: 1.5, key: 2, fill: .5, rim: .6 }, floor: \"none\", shadows: true },\n  { id: \"review\", name: \"Review sheet\", ground: \"#e9edf0\", ink: \"#25313a\", faint: \"#566773\", accent: \"#305d83\", surface: { color: \"#f7f9fa\", roughness: 1, glows: false }, lights: { ambient: 1.5, key: 2, fill: .5, rim: .6 }, floor: \"none\", shadows: true },\n] as const satisfies readonly Look[];\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/content/story.ts",
      "sha256": "ddfaa151e46c390a540b4c96a4ca81e0bf30ee677a16f67d9b4152ebd78fa32e",
      "bytes": 621,
      "text": "import type { StoryDef, BeatIdOf } from \"../lib/story/story.ts\";\n// Stop 4: the three approved spine beats. The remaining four join after look review.\nexport const story = {\n  pieces: 6,\n  chapters: [\n    { id: \"scope\", number: 1, name: \"Scope\", role: \"opening\", beats: [{ id: \"scope.opening\", height: 1 }] },\n    { id: \"transition\", number: 4, name: \"Transition\", role: \"how\", beats: [{ id: \"transition.diagram\", height: 1.5 }] },\n    { id: \"review\", number: 6, name: \"Review\", role: \"close\", beats: [{ id: \"review.close\", height: 1 }] },\n  ],\n} as const satisfies StoryDef;\nexport type BeatId = BeatIdOf<typeof story>;\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/docs/story/check-results.json",
      "sha256": "fa504e70401ecee1de795f648a41c63b6528717c3744f5fffd806a9987e4e59d",
      "bytes": 17381,
      "text": "{\n  \"run\": \"checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z\",\n  \"complete\": true,\n  \"scope\": \"full matrix\",\n  \"node\": \"v22.22.1\",\n  \"commands\": [\n    [\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-node-check-20261011/node_modules/node/bin/node\",\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/node_modules/next/dist/bin/next\",\n      \"build\"\n    ],\n    [\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-node-check-20261011/node_modules/node/bin/node\",\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/node_modules/next/dist/bin/next\",\n      \"start\",\n      \"--hostname\",\n      \"127.0.0.1\",\n      \"--port\",\n      \"37313\"\n    ],\n    [\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-node-check-20261011/node_modules/node/bin/node\",\n \"--expect-ids=scope.opening,transition.diagram,review.close\",\n      \"--beats\",\n      \"--inside=.story-control\",\n      \"--expect-look=light\",\n      \"--size=1440x900\",\n      \"--expect-scene=on\",\n      \"--positions=start,mid,end\",\n      \"--late-shaders=40000\",\n      \"--wait=1000\"\n    ],\n    [\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-node-check-20261011/node_modules/node/bin/node\",\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/scripts/shoot.mjs\",\n      \"--url=http://127.0.0.1:37313/?look=light\",\n      \"--out=/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z/light-short\",\n      \"--report=/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z/light-short/report.json\",\n      \"--expect-beats=3\",\n      \"--expect-ids=scope.opening,transition.diagram,review.close\",\n      \"--beats\",\n      \"--inside=.story-control\",\n      \"--expect-look=light\",\n      \"--size=1280x720\",\n      \"--expect-scene=on\",\n      \"--late-shaders=40000\",\n      \"--wait=1000\"\n    ],\n    [\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-node-check-20261011/node_modules/node/bin/node\",\n      \"/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/scripts/shoot.mjs\",\n      \"--url=http://127.0.0.1:37313/?look=light\",\n      \"--out=/tmp/storyfolio-evals/build-embedded-1-GoHJ5r/checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z/light-phone\",\n       "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/docs/story/decided-for-you.md",
      "sha256": "342ed96f07031482bccfb8a816f6c255f6cbed15354ba86c8e40be217d5ddadb",
      "bytes": 5213,
      "text": "# Scope decisions\n\nThese decisions are coordinator-authored fictional confirmations for the\namended build. They do not retroactively change the historical run. The\nperson's new confirmation is in `confirmations.md`; source limitations\nremain in `facts.md`.\n\n| Site checklist item | Disposition | Who decided and why |\n|---|---|---|\n| Way around the page | Build: six chapter anchors, skip-to-content and skip-to-contact | Historical planner; derivative confirms short visits and keyboard access. |\n| A project's own page | Never in this fixture: one complete Mosslink case on the main page | Historical planner; fictional derivative approves this explicit exception. It does not test general project-page support. |\n| Every project listed | Build: Mosslink, the only supplied project | Source scope and fictional confirmation; no invented project grid. |\n| Contact and CV | Build: `mailto:ada@example.com` and corrected downloadable HTML CV at opening and close | Fictional confirmation resolves address and format; mailbox delivery is not tested. |\n| What others say | Never in this fixture | Historical person supplied no testimonials; do not invent them. |\n| Languages | Build: English only; later if translations are supplied | Historical planner and derivative source language. |\n| Shared-link preview and icon | Build: truthful title/summary, supplied static share-card SVG and icon; record platform format limits | Derivative coordinator authored local assets; social-platform compatibility is not claimed without a validated raster derivative and platform check. |\n| Missing address | Build: readable 404 with Home and Email Ada links | Historical planner; useful even for a one-page scope. |\n| Blog | Never in this fixture | No writing or request supplied. |\n| Chat | Never in this fixture | Person requested email and CV, no chat. |\n| Legal notice where required | Later, before any publication | Hosting jurisdiction and legal particulars are absent. Local evaluation authorizes no publishing or legal-compliance claim. |\n\n## Other explicit choices\n\n- Keep the selected six-row State by state form, six chapters and seven\n  beats. Eight desktop viewports are a budget, not a reason for blank\n  mobile screens. This is a card regression, not a new object-family proof.\n- Replace the missing fixture photo with an honestly labelled explanatory\n  schematic. Use the supplied teaching diagram without claiming real\n  firmware topology or measured timing. No private circuitry or trace.\n- Use system sans-serif and monospace fonts because no licensed named\n  font files are supplied. Keep all three appearances available for the\n  spine's actual choice and subsequent review.\n- Use static HTML and drawings on narrow screens, under reduced motion,\n  without JavaScript or without working WebGL. Normal desktop still uses\n  a six-piece scroll scene.\n- Include no unconfirmed employment chronology, incident resolution,\n  influence story, certification, field results or extra projects.\n- Keep source and permission records, test results and handover notes in\n  the local project. Publishing and external messages remain outside scope.\n\n## Stop 4 implementation decisions — 11 October 2026\n\n- The current working page contains only Scope, Transition and Review, as\n  requested for the spine review. Their IDs and chapter numbers match the\n  approved full storyboard. Four remaining beats, their copy and the six\n  chapter navigation are reserved for stop 5 after the look choice.\n- All three appearances use the identical six rows and formations. Query\n  values are `light` (Bench paper, default), `dark` (Instrument paper), and\n  `review` (Review sheet). Change palettes in `content/looks.ts`.\n- Use an ordinary local SVG preview and expansion link alongside the opening\n  and transition text. A compact desktop preview expands to a large static\n  illustration when the scene is off. This keeps its explanatory limit next\n  to the image. Change presentation in `app/globals.css`.\n- Narrow and reduced-motion visitors never mount the dynamic scene. The\n  browser matrix declares this fixture's static choices explicitly rather\n  than retaining the engine's scene-on expectation for those cases.\n- Keep the look switch available while scrolling. No look has been chosen\n  by the person yet. No surrounding-copy choice is inferred from approval\n  of the factual sentences.\n- Row textures declare their printed aspect ratio so the GPU does not\n  downsample a portrait texture into blurred wide text. The engine keeps its\n  original default for other bundles; new ratios are bounded by validation.\n- Static header navigation is positioned against the story root, outside\n  the content's top spacing. Screenshot review found and corrected its\n  initial overlap with the opening on phone.\n- The environment terminated the long sequential browser command before\n  the full matrix completed. The checker now accepts optional `--jobs=3`\n  to run independent cases in batches on the same production server; its\n  default is still sequential. Full scope and all failure checks remain.\n  `--wait=1000` is the final screenshot settle interval. No selected-case or\n  skip-build run is presented as a full acceptance run.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/docs/story/handover.md",
      "sha256": "5727c0fedc15f1070e85a87643587509716659052caf812ddcf208c7c502aa65",
      "bytes": 5688,
      "text": "# Stop 4: local spine for appearance review\n\n## Person's words and current gate\n\nOn 11 October 2026 the user said yes to the derivative `storyboard.md` and\nfictional `confirmations.md`, explicitly authorized building beyond the\nStoryfolio skill's planning-only ending, and asked: “First build the spine:\nthe opening, a representative middle chapter and the ending, in two or\nthree looks for the same approved object. Let me scroll the working result\nand choose a look before completing the rest.” Everything stays local.\n\nNo concept selection was restarted. The selected State by state object is\nsix matte narrative row cards. These are neither six firmware states nor\nsix physical boards. The skill was read; the user's explicit continuation\nsupersedes its planning-only ending. No look choice or later copy approval\nhas been fabricated.\n\n## Implemented\n\nThe plain starter has been replaced with three stable spine beats:\n`scope.opening`, `transition.diagram`, `review.close`. Original chapter\nnumbers 1, 4 and 6 remain visible. Six persistent rows gain shallow depth,\nseparate into three labelled teaching-loop pairs, then reassemble. All\nthree looks use the same geometry, words and scroll progression.\n\nBench paper is the default (`?look=light` or no query). Instrument paper\nuses `?look=dark`; Review sheet uses `?look=review`. The switch preserves\nscroll, fragment and other query parameters. The palette lives in\n`content/looks.ts`; formations and face drawings live beside it.\n\nServer-rendered English text contains the approved opening definition,\nAda's contribution, diagram limits and closing answer with projection,\n72-hour run and unfinished qualification limits. The two local explanatory\nSVGs are shown and linked, with their recorded alternative text. Email and\ndownloadable corrected HTML CV are available in the header, opening and\nclose. A local missing-page view has Home and Email Ada links.\n\nStatic modes use readable single-column text and large diagrams. At\n767 CSS pixels or less, and with reduced motion at every width, the dynamic\nscene never mounts. No JavaScript, no WebGL or renderer failure keeps the\nspine text, diagrams and links. The current spine has three beats; the\ncomplete seven-beat static contract becomes applicable when stop 5 is built.\n\nEngine changes are limited to the static-mode gate, optional bounded face\naspect ratios for legible wide row textures, and explicit check-matrix\nstatic-mode declarations. Optional starter extras and piece-overlay links\nare not used by this spine. Existing engine defaults remain for other\nbundles. System sans-serif and monospace fonts require no external service.\n\n## Environment and validation\n\nActual inspection found a supplied Next.js 16.3.8 starter and locked\npackages, not a prior user portfolio. Node is v22.22.1. `npm ci` hit a\nblocked registry request; `npm ci --offline` successfully installed all\n392 locked packages from the available cache. This is not a fresh advisory\nreview. `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH=/tmp/storyfolio-browser-cache npm run\nbrowsers` passed. Browser runtime libraries were supplied locally via\n`LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/tmp/storyfolio-browser-libs/root/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu`.\n\nRead the installed Next.js 16 documentation for dynamic client loading,\nmetadata, missing routes and Link. The share SVG remains a local source\nasset: social-platform acceptance of SVG previews has not been tested.\nMetadata currently resolves against localhost; an actual publication URL\nand legal details are prepublication work.\n\n`npm test`: 131 tests passed. `npm run typecheck` and `npm run lint`: passed.\nThe final fresh production browser run is under\n`checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z/`; all 18 browser cases passed after a fresh build using `--jobs=3 --wait=1000`.\nVisual review is recorded in `review.md`, and compact results are committed\nin `check-results.json`. Earlier interrupted runs are diagnostic only.\nScreenshot review corrected stretched/blurred row lettering, static\nheader overlap and the shorter-desktop appearance-switch position.\n\n`scripts/check-spine.mjs` additionally checks actual scene chunk requests,\nstatic phone/reduced modes, desktop deep-link and chapter-navigation state,\nkeyboard reachability, email/CV attributes, all supplied assets and the\nmissing-route response and links. It writes its results and extra screenshots\nunder the final run's `supplemental/` folder.\n\n## Local use and repository history\n\nRun `NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 npm run dev` from this project and open the\nprinted local address. Scroll each appearance before choosing one. No site\nhas been published and no email has been sent.\n\nThis sandbox exposes an empty read-only `.git` placeholder, so ordinary\ngit cannot create repository history there. Local history is stored in\n`.storyfolio-git/` inside this project instead. Use\n`git --git-dir=.storyfolio-git --work-tree=. status` (and the same prefix for\nlog/diff/commit). It is ignored as a working-tree path. Only known project\nsources and story records are included; injected credential placeholders,\ndependencies, builds and screenshots are excluded.\n\n## Next\n\nWait for the person's scroll-based choice among Bench paper, Instrument\npaper and Review sheet. Then implement Responsibilities, State and both\nObservation beats from the approved storyboard, preserving all seven IDs,\nsix chapter anchors and eleven exact confirmed sentences. Offer opening\nand closing surrounding-copy choices at stop 5. Do not infer those choices\nfrom approval of the storyboard or facts. Finish checks only after that\nreview. Automated Chromium checks and fictional confirmations are not\nreal-phone performance evidence or a real repository author's review.\n"

      "text": "# Spine review — ready for the person's look choice\n\n11 October 2026. This is stop 4, not approval of the completed site.\n\nThe local working spine contains Ada Mensah, Reading transitions and Code\nbeside evidence, carried by the same six row cards in all three looks.\nUse the appearance switch to compare Bench paper, Instrument paper and\nReview sheet while scrolling. No choice has yet been received.\n\n## Checks performed\n\n| Command / inspection | Result |\n|---|---|\n| `npm ci` | Registry request blocked; installation recovered with `npm ci --offline` (392 locked packages) |\n| `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH=/tmp/storyfolio-browser-cache npm run browsers` | Passed |\n| `npm test` | 131 passed, 0 failed |\n| `npm run typecheck` | Passed |\n| `npm run lint` | Passed |\n| `npm run check -- --jobs=3 --wait=1000` with supplied browser cache and libraries | Fresh production build; full matrix, 18/18 passed |\n| `node scripts/check-spine.mjs checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z/supplemental` with the same browser environment | Static chunk loading, desktop navigation/deep link, keyboard reachability, supplied assets, email/CV attributes and missing-page checks passed |\n| Visual inspection | All 93 matrix screenshots opened through labelled contact sheets; representative original desktop and phone images also opened. Supplemental screenshots opened separately. |\n\nProduction evidence: `checks/2026-10-11T01-55-22-591Z/`. Its manifest is\ncomplete and reports no browser problems. The tracked compact record is\n`check-results.json`; logs, individual PNGs and labelled contact sheets\nremain locally under that check folder. Previous runs were stopped for\nvisual corrections or terminated before completion; they are not acceptance\nruns. The final checker still defaults to sequential execution; optional\nbatching preserved the entire matrix within the environment's runtime limit.\n\n## Observations\n\nThe desktop table stays framed beside the prose, separates into Sleep,\nMeasure and Transmit teaching pairs, then returns to its row formation.\nThe third appearance changes the palette to cool paper and blue annotations;\nit keeps the same object and factual labels. All looks keep readable text\nand labelled explanatory drawings. The short-desktop switch is above the\nrows. Phone header, chapter links, switch and opening now have separate\nspace. Large static diagrams are readable without the canvas.\n\nPhone and reduced-motion contexts each mounted zero canvases and requested\nzero identified scene chunks. Desktop mounted one canvas and fetched that\nchunk. Direct arrival at `#transition` and navigation to `#review` settled\nwith the matching active scene chapter. Switching appearances preserved the\nfragment, other query parameters and scroll in the matrix. Context loss\nremoved the scene and retained the HTML presentation.\n\nInitial screenshot inspection found stretched/blurred row text, a static\nheader overlap, and a short-screen control overlap. These were corrected\nbefore the final complete run. Static snapshots can start partway through a\nsection because the engine samples scroll rests; the full text remains in\nordinary document flow and is reachable by scrolling.\n\n## Scope and limits\n\nThe remaining four beats and their core sentences are intentionally deferred\nuntil the person chooses a look. The full seven-beat content contract has\nnot yet been completed or approved. No fictional response is counted as\nreal scrolling review. No real phone performance, mail delivery, legal\nadequacy, deployment or external social-card compatibility is established.\nThe corrected CV is HTML. The two diagrams are explanatory SVGs, not\nphotographs, circuitry, measured traces or specifications.\n\n## Awaiting\n\nThe person's choice: Bench paper, Instrument paper or Review sheet. After\nthat, continue stop 5 without restarting the concept: add Responsibilities,\nState and both Observation beats, keep all eleven confirmed sentences and\nsix chapter links, and offer the requested surrounding-copy choices.\n"

    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/scripts/check-lib.mjs",
      "sha256": "e32668b8d452c4153b16fd5bd36ae11f2112f66bf0383ed8b88f755ed0d83c07",
      "bytes": 4710,
      "text": "import { TRAVEL } from \"../lib/story/timeline.ts\";\n\n/** Empty ignore values mean no exception. A nonempty value is an explicit regex. */\nexport function optionalPattern(value) {\n  return value ? new RegExp(String(value)) : null;\n}\n\nexport function expectedRequestReason({ url, resourceType, failure }, { ignore = null, block = null, noJavaScript = false, pageUrl } = {}) {\n  if (block?.test(url)) return \"intentionally blocked request\";\n  if (ignore?.test(url)) return \"explicit ignore pattern\";\n  if (noJavaScript && resourceType === \"script\" && [\"csp\", \"net::ERR_BLOCKED_BY_CSP\"].includes(failure)) {\n    try {\n      const request = new URL(url);\n      if (request.origin === new URL(pageUrl).origin && /^\\/_next\\/static\\/.+\\.js$/.test(request.pathname)) {\n        return \"JavaScript-disabled CSP rejection of a same-origin Next.js script preload\";\n      }\n    } catch { /* Invalid URLs cannot qualify for this narrow exception. */ }\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport function requestProblem({ url, status, failure, resourceType }, options = {}) {\n  if (expectedRequestReason({ url, resourceType, failure }, options)) return null;\n  if (failure) return `request failed (${failure}): ${url}`;\n  return status >= 400 ? `HTTP ${status}: ${url}` : null;\n}\n\nexport function storyContract(story, looks) {\n  const ids = story.chapters.flatMap(chapter => chapter.beats.map(beat => beat.id));\n  if (!ids.length || new Set(ids).size !== ids.length || ids.some(id => !/^[\\w.-]+$/.test(id))) {\n    throw new Error(\"The check contract needs unique, nonempty beat IDs\");\n  }\n  const lookIds = looks.map(look => look.id);\n  if (!lookIds.length || new Set(lookIds).size !== lookIds.length) throw new Error(\"The check contract needs unique look IDs\");\n  return { ids, lookIds };\n}\n\n/** The contract comes from content, never from whatever the first browser happened to render. */\nexport function checkMatrix({ ids, lookIds }, probes = {}) {\n  const common = [`--expect-beats=${ids.length}`, `--expect-ids=${ids.join(\",\")}`, \"--beats\", \"--inside=.story-control\"];\n  const matrix = [];\n  for (const look of lookIds) {\n    const expected = [...common, `--expect-look=${look}`];\n    matrix.push(\n      { name: `${look}-wide`, look, flags: [...expected, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--expect-scene=on\", \"--positions=start,mid,end\", \"--late-shaders=40000\"] },\n      { name: `${look}-short`, look, flags: [...expected, \"--size=1280x720\", \"--expect-scene=on\", \"--late-shaders=40000\"] },\n      { name: `${look}-phone`, look, flags: [...expected, \"--size=390x844\", \"--touch\", probes.staticPhone ? \"--expect-scene=off\" : \"--expect-scene=on\", ...(probes.staticPhone ? [\"--hidden=canvas\"] : [])] },\n      { name: `${look}-reduced`, look, flags: [...expected, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--reduced-motion\", probes.staticReduced ? \"--expect-scene=off\" : \"--expect-scene=on\", \"--positions=start,mid,end\", ...(probes.staticReduced ? [\"--hidden=canvas\"] : [\"--assert-still=300\"])] },\n      { name: `${look}-no-webgl`, look, flags: [...expected, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--no-webgl\", \"--expect-scene=off\"] },\n    );\n  }\n  matrix.push(\n    { name: \"no-js\", look: lookIds[0], flags: [...common, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--no-js\", \"--expect-selector=h1\", \"--hidden=canvas,.story-look-switch,.story-control\"] },\n    { name: \"context-loss\", look: lookIds[0], flags: [...common, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--lose-context\", \"--expect-scene=off\"] },\n  );\n  if (lookIds.length > 1) matrix.push({ name: \"look-switch\", look: lookIds[0], flags: [...common, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--expect-scene=on\", `--look-cycle=${lookIds.slice(0, 2).join(\",\")}`, `--expect-look=${lookIds[0]}`] });\n  if (probes.overlay) {\n    if (!ids.includes(probes.overlay.beat) || !probes.overlay.selector) throw new Error(\"The overlay probe needs a declared beat and selector\");\n    matrix.push({ name: \"keyboard-overlay\", look: lookIds[0], flags: [...common, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--expect-scene=on\", `--keyboard=${probes.overlay.selector}`, `--keyboard-at=${probes.overlay.beat}`] });\n  }\n  if (probes.lazyExtra) matrix.push({ name: \"failed-extra\", look: lookIds[0], flags: [...common, \"--size=1440x900\", \"--expect-scene=on\", \"--block-late-chunks\"] });\n  return matrix;\n}\n/** Match playhead's rest intervals, including arrival before later blocks reach the top. */\nexport function beatRestSamples(boxes, viewport, positions = [\"end\"]) {\n  let arrived = 0;\n  const travel = Math.max(1, TRAVEL * viewport);\n  return boxes.map(box => {\n    const leaves = Math.max(arrived, box.top + box.height - viewport);\n    const samples = positions.map(position => ({\n      position,\n      top: arrived + (leaves - arrived) * ({ start: 0, mid: 0.5, end: 1 }[position]),\n    }));\n    arrived = leaves + travel;\n    return samples;\n  });\n}\n\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/scripts/check-lib.test.mjs",
      "sha256": "c22b87f0a7461a84ad20fb33c47022615223cc1f7e7542e28004c2d48c452e10",
      "bytes": 4805,
      "text": "import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport test from \"node:test\";\nimport { beatRestSamples, checkMatrix, expectedRequestReason, optionalPattern, requestProblem, storyContract } from \"./check-lib.mjs\";\nimport { playhead } from \"../lib/story/timeline.ts\";\n\ntest(\"later tall beats sample their whole dwell from arrival, not just their block top\", () => {\n  const boxes = [{ top: 0, height: 2000 }, { top: 2000, height: 2000 }, { top: 4000, height: 1000 }];\n  const samples = beatRestSamples(boxes, 1000, [\"start\", \"mid\", \"end\"]);\n  assert.deepEqual(samples[1].map(sample => sample.top), [1400, 2200, 3000]);\n  for (let i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) {\n    for (const [position, sample] of samples[i].entries()) {\n      const head = playhead(sample.top, 1000, boxes);\n      assert.equal(head.beat, i);\n      assert.equal(head.dwell, position / 2);\n    }\n  }\n});\n\ntest(\"empty request ignore does not suppress HTTP errors or failures without responses\", () => {\n  const ignore = optionalPattern(\"\");\n  assert.equal(ignore, null);\n  assert.match(requestProblem({ url: \"http://localhost/chunk.js\", status: 404 }, { ignore }), /HTTP 404/);\n  assert.match(requestProblem({ url: \"http://localhost/chunk.js\", failure: \"net::ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED\" }, { ignore }), /ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED/);\n  assert.equal(requestProblem({ url: \"http://localhost/image.svg\", status: 200 }), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"only explicitly blocked or ignored requests are omitted\", () => {\n  const block = optionalPattern(\"/extra\\\\.js$\");\n  assert.equal(requestProblem({ url: \"http://localhost/extra.js\", failure: \"net::ERR_FAILED\" }, { block }), null);\n  assert.match(requestProblem({ url: \"http://localhost/other.js\", failure: \"net::ERR_FAILED\" }, { block }), /other\\.js/);\n});\n\ntest(\"only disabled-JavaScript CSP failures for same-origin Next scripts are expected\", () => {\n  const options = { noJavaScript: true, pageUrl: \"http://localhost:3000/?look=light\" };\n  const script = { url: \"http://localhost:3000/_next/static/chunks/app.js\", resourceType: \"script\", failure: \"csp\" };\n  for (const failure of [\"csp\", \"net::ERR_BLOCKED_BY_CSP\"]) {\n    assert.equal(requestProblem({ ...script, failure }, options), null);\n    assert.match(expectedRequestReason({ ...script, failure }, options), /JavaScript-disabled CSP/);\n  }\n  const unexpected = [\n    { ...script, resourceType: \"image\" },\n    { ...script, resourceType: \"stylesheet\" },\n    { ...script, url: \"http://other.test/_next/static/chunks/app.js\" },\n    { ...script, url: \"http://localhost:3000/custom.js\" },\n    { ...script, failure: \"net::ERR_CONNECTION_REFUSED\" },\n    { ...script, failure: \"net::ERR_ABORTED\" },\n    { ...script, failure: undefined, status: 404 },\n  ];\n  for (const request of unexpected) assert.ok(requestProblem(request, options), JSON.stringify(request));\n  assert.ok(requestProblem(script, { ...options, noJavaScript: false }));\n});\n\ntest(\"every matrix case expects the declared IDs, including a beat missing from every rendering\", () => {\n  const contract = storyContract({ chapters: [{ beats: [{ id: \"opening\" }, { id: \"missing\" }, { id: \"close\" }] }] }, [{ id: \"light\" }, { id: \"dark\" }]);\n  for (const entry of checkMatrix(contract)) {\n    assert.ok(entry.flags.includes(\"--expect-beats=3\"));\n    assert.ok(entry.flags.includes(\"--expect-ids=opening,missing,close\"));\n  }\n  assert.throws(() => storyContract({ chapters: [{ beats: [{ id: \"same\" }, { id: \"same\" }] }] }, [{ id: \"light\" }]), /unique/);\n});\n\ntest(\"fixture-specific probes require explicit content contracts\", () => {\n  const contract = { ids: [\"opening\", \"close\"], lookIds: [\"light\"] };\n  assert.ok(checkMatrix(contract).every(entry => ![\"failed-extra\", \"keyboard-overlay\"].includes(entry.name)));\n  const matrix = checkMatrix(contract, { overlay: { beat: \"close\", selector: \".link\" }, lazyExtra: true });\n  assert.ok(matrix.some(entry => entry.name === \"failed-extra\"));\n  assert.ok(matrix.find(entry => entry.name === \"keyboard-overlay\").flags.includes(\"--keyboard-at=close\"));\n  assert.throws(() => checkMatrix(contract, { overlay: { beat: \"missing\", selector: \".link\" } }), /declared beat/);\n});\n\ntest(\"static fixture modes require no scene while the default engine retains it\", () => {\n  const contract = { ids: [\"opening\"], lookIds: [\"light\"] };\n  const defaultMatrix = checkMatrix(contract);\n  const staticMatrix = checkMatrix(contract, { staticPhone: true, staticReduced: true });\n  for (const mode of [\"phone\", \"reduced\"]) {\n    assert.ok(defaultMatrix.find(entry => entry.name === `light-${mode}`).flags.includes(\"--expect-scene=on\"));\n    const flags = staticMatrix.find(entry => entry.name === `light-${mode}`).flags;\n    assert.ok(flags.includes(\"--expect-scene=off\"));\n    assert.ok(flags.includes(\"--hidden=canvas\"));\n    assert.ok(!flags.includes(\"--assert-still=300\"));\n  }\n});\n"
    },
