      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/scripts/check-spine.mjs",
      "sha256": "441e7ee332c3a6b6501dcc82b37fbce54fdc598c201306dd000660c940f40b3c",
      "bytes": 4139,
      "text": "// Stop-4 supplemental checks. Use a freshly built app after npm run check.\nimport { chromium } from 'playwright-core';\nimport { spawn } from 'node:child_process';\nimport { createServer } from 'node:net';\nimport { readFile, readdir, mkdir, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nconst output = process.argv[2] ?? 'checks/spine-supplemental';\nawait mkdir(output, { recursive: true });\nconst socket = createServer();\nawait new Promise(ok => socket.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', ok));\nconst port = socket.address().port;\nawait new Promise(ok => socket.close(ok));\nconst server = spawn(process.execPath, ['node_modules/next/dist/bin/next', 'start', '--hostname', '127.0.0.1', '--port', String(port)], { env: { ...process.env, NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: '1' }, stdio: 'ignore' });\nconst base = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}`;\nlet browser;\nconst results = [];\ntry {\n  for (let i=0; i<100; i++) {\n    try { if ((await fetch(base)).ok) break; } catch {}\n    await new Promise(ok => setTimeout(ok, 100));\n  }\n  const sceneChunks = [];\n  for (const file of await readdir('.next/static/chunks')) {\n    if (file.endsWith('.js') && /story-face-v1|WebGLRenderer/.test(await readFile(`.next/static/chunks/${file}`, 'utf8'))) sceneChunks.push(file);\n  }\n  assert.ok(sceneChunks.length, 'identify actual scene chunk');\n  browser = await chromium.launch({ args: ['--no-sandbox', '--use-angle=swiftshader', '--enable-unsafe-swiftshader', '--ignore-gpu-blocklist'] });\n  for (const mode of ['phone', 'reduced', 'desktop']) {\n    const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: mode==='phone' ? { width:390, height:844 } : { width:1440, height:900 }, reducedMotion: mode==='reduced' ? 'reduce' : 'no-preference' });\n    const page = await context.newPage(); const requests=[];\n    page.on('request', request => requests.push(request.url()));\n    await page.goto(`${base}/`, { waitUntil:'networkidle' });\n    await page.screenshot({ path:`${output}/${mode}-initial.png` });\n    await page.goto(`${base}/#transition`, { waitUntil:'networkidle' }); await page.waitForTimeout(2500);\n    const canvas = await page.locator('canvas').count();\n    const requestedScene = requests.filter(url => sceneChunks.some(chunk => url.endsWith('/'+chunk)));\n    if (mode==='desktop') {\n      assert.equal(canvas,1); assert.ok(requestedScene.length);\n      assert.equal(await page.locator('.story-rail a[aria-current]').getAttribute('href'), '#transition');\n      await page.screenshot({ path:`${output}/desktop-deep-transition.png` });\n      await page.locator('.story-rail a[href=\"#review\"]').click(); await page.waitForTimeout(2500);\n      assert.equal(await page.locator('.story-rail a[aria-current]').getAttribute('href'),'#review');\n      await page.keyboard.press('Tab');\n      assert.ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.activeElement?.tagName === 'A' || document.activeElement?.tagName === 'BUTTON'));\n    } else { assert.equal(canvas,0); assert.deepEqual(requestedScene,[]); }\n    assert.equal(await page.locator('h1').count(),1);\n    for (const path of ['/assets/power-states.svg','/assets/bring-up-fixture.svg','/assets/share-card.svg','/icon.svg','/ada-mensah-cv.html']) assert.equal((await context.request.get(base+path)).status(),200);\n    assert.equal(await page.locator('a[download]').count(),3);\n    assert.equal(await page.locator('a[href=\"mailto:ada@example.com\"]').count(),3);\n    assert.equal((await context.request.get(`${base}/missing-address`)).status(),404);\n    await page.goto(`${base}/missing-address`); assert.equal(await page.getByRole('link',{name:'Home',exact:true}).getAttribute('href'),'/');\n    assert.equal(await page.getByRole('link',{name:'Email Ada',exact:true}).getAttribute('href'),'mailto:ada@example.com');\n    results.push({mode,canvas,requestedScene,passed:true}); await context.close();\n  }\n  await writeFile(`${output}/report.json`, JSON.stringify({ sceneChunks, results, limits:'Automated Chromium checks; no real phone, mail delivery, publication or social-platform validation.' },null,2)+'\\n');\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(results));\n} finally { await browser?.close(); server.kill('SIGTERM'); }\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "run/turns/01/snapshot/files/scripts/check.mjs",
      "sha256": "0fb7f65d1bf3cd62a98574c749b015e5a07b1d9e10888686dfefd4342bb1a064",
      "bytes": 6615,
      "text": "#!/usr/bin/env node\nimport { spawn } from \"node:child_process\";\nimport { createWriteStream } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from \"node:fs/promises\";\nimport { createRequire } from \"node:module\";\nimport { createServer } from \"node:net\";\nimport { dirname, join, resolve } from \"node:path\";\nimport { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from \"node:url\";\nimport { checkMatrix, storyContract } from \"./check-lib.mjs\";\n\nconst root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \"..\");\nconst options = Object.fromEntries(process.argv.slice(2).map(arg => {\n  const [key, ...rest] = arg.replace(/^--/, \"\").split(\"=\");\n  return [key, rest.length ? rest.join(\"=\") : true];\n}));\nconst output = resolve(root, String(options.out ?? `checks/${new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, \"-\")}`));\nawait mkdir(output, { recursive: true });\nconst require = createRequire(import.meta.url);\nconst next = require.resolve(\"next/dist/bin/next\");\nconst [{ story }, { looks }] = await Promise.all([\n  import(pathToFileURL(join(root, \"content/story.ts\")).href),\n  import(pathToFileURL(join(root, \"content/looks.ts\")).href),\n]);\nconst contract = storyContract(story, looks);\nconst probes = await readFile(join(root, \"content/checks.json\"), \"utf8\").then(JSON.parse).catch(error => {\n  if (error.code === \"ENOENT\") return {};\n  throw error;\n});\nconst matrix = checkMatrix(contract, probes).filter(entry => !options.only || String(options.only).split(\",\").includes(entry.name));\nif (!matrix.length) throw new Error(\"--only selected no checks\");\nconst manifest = { started: new Date().toISOString(), node: process.version, scope: options.only ? \"selected cases\" : \"full matrix\", contract, probes, matrix, commands: [], results: [], complete: false };\nconst save = () => writeFile(join(output, \"manifest.json\"), JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2) + \"\\n\");\nawait save();\nconst children = new Set();\nlet cancelled = false;\n\nfunction stop(child, signal = \"SIGTERM\") {\n  if (!child?.pid || child.exitCode !== null) return;\n  try {\n    if (process.platform === \"win32\") child.kill(signal);\n    else process.kill(-child.pid, signal);\n  }\n  catch (error) { if (error.code !== \"ESRCH\") throw error; }\n}\n\nfunction launch(args, logName) {\n  const log = createWriteStream(join(output, logName));\n  const child = spawn(process.execPath, args, {\n    cwd: root, detached: process.platform !== \"win32\",\n    env: { ...process.env, NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED: \"1\" }, stdio: [\"ignore\", \"pipe\", \"pipe\"],\n  });\n  children.add(child);\n  manifest.commands.push([process.execPath, ...args]);\n  child.stdout.on(\"data\", chunk => log.write(chunk));\n  child.stderr.on(\"data\", chunk => log.write(chunk));\n  const done = new Promise(resolveDone => {\n    child.on(\"error\", error => { log.write(`${error.stack}\\n`); });\n    child.on(\"close\", (code, signal) => {\n      children.delete(child);\n      log.end(() => resolveDone({ code, signal }));\n    });\n  });\n  return { child, done };\n}\n\nasync function run(args, logName, timeout = 180_000) {\n  const processRun = launch(args, logName);\n  const timer = setTimeout(() => stop(processRun.child), timeout);\n  const hardStop = setTimeout(() => stop(processRun.child, \"SIGKILL\"), timeout + 5000);\n  try { return await processRun.done; }\n  finally { clearTimeout(timer); clearTimeout(hardStop); }\n}\n\nasync function freePort() {\n  const server = createServer();\n  await new Promise((ok, fail) => { server.once(\"error\", fail); server.listen(0, \"127.0.0.1\", ok); });\n  const port = server.address().port;\n  await new Promise((ok, fail) => server.close(error => error ? fail(error) : ok()));\n  return port;\n}\n\nasync function waitForServer(url, child) {\n  const deadline = Date.now() + 45_000;\n  while (Date.now() < deadline) {\n    if (child.exitCode !== null) throw new Error(\"Next.js server exited before readiness; read server.log\");\n    try { if ((await fetch(url, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(1000) })).status === 200) return; }\n    catch { /* The local server may still be starting. */ }\n    await new Promise(ok => setTimeout(ok, 250));\n  }\n  throw new Error(\"Next.js did not return HTTP 200 within 45 seconds\");\n}\n\nfor (const signal of [\"SIGINT\", \"SIGTERM\"]) process.on(signal, () => {\n  cancelled = true;\n  for (const child of children) stop(child, signal);\n  process.exitCode = 1;\n});\n\nlet server;\ntry {\n  if (!options[\"skip-build\"]) {\n    console.log(\"Building the production app\");\n    const result = await run([next, \"build\"], \"build.log\");\n    if (result.code !== 0) throw new Error(`Production build failed (${result.code}); read build.log`);\n  }\n  const port = await freePort();\n  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${port}/`;\n  server = launch([next, \"start\", \"--hostname\", \"127.0.0.1\", \"--port\", String(port)], \"server.log\");\n  await waitForServer(base, server.child);\n  const jobs = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, Math.floor(Number(options.jobs ?? 1)) || 1));\n  for (let offset = 0; offset < matrix.length; offset += jobs) {\n    const batch = await Promise.all(matrix.slice(offset, offset + jobs).map(async entry => {\n    if (cancelled) throw new Error(\"Check run interrupted; remaining cases were not started\");\n    console.log(`Checking ${entry.name}`);\n    const directory = join(output, entry.name);\n    await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });\n    const reportPath = join(directory, \"report.json\");\n    const args = [join(root, \"scripts/shoot.mjs\"), `--url=${base}?look=${encodeURIComponent(entry.look)}`, `--out=${directory}`, `--report=${reportPath}`, ...entry.flags];\n    if (options.wait) args.push(`--wait=${options.wait}`);\n    const result = await run(args, `${entry.name}.log`, 240_000);\n    const report = await readFile(reportPath, \"utf8\").then(JSON.parse).catch(() => null);\n    return { name: entry.name, ...result, report: report ? `${entry.name}/report.json` : null, problems: report?.problems ?? [\"No browser report; read this case's log\"] };\n    }));\n    manifest.results.push(...batch);\n    await save();\n  }\n  manifest.complete = true;\n  manifest.finished = new Date().toISOString();\n  await save();\n  const failed = manifest.results.filter(result => result.code !== 0 || result.problems.length);\n  console.log(`${matrix.length - failed.length}/${matrix.length} checks passed. Evidence: ${output}`);\n  if (failed.length) process.exitCode = 1;\n} catch (error) {\n  manifest.error = error.stack;\n  await save();\n  console.error(error.message);\n  process.exitCode = 1;\n} finally {\n  if (server) {\n    stop(server.child);\n    const timer = setTimeout(() => stop(server.child, \"SIGKILL\"), 5000);\n    await server.done;\n    clearTimeout(timer);\n  }\n  for (const child of children) stop(child);\n}\n"
    }
  ]
}
